p\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
, one has

\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x)\,dx=\frac{1}{2}.

Proof.

We define the function

D_{p,n}(x)\coloneqq\frac{p^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{2\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\arctan\left(\frac{x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}}{\frac{2p}{n}}\right).

Then

\displaystyle D_{p,n}^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=\frac{p^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{2\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\frac{\frac{nx}{p}}{1+\left(\frac{x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}}{\frac{2p}{n}}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{px}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\frac{2p^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}=\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x).

Since

\arctan\left(\frac{\left(p\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}}{\frac{2p}{n}}\right)=\arctan\left(\frac{\frac{1}{n}\pm\frac{2p}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}}{\frac{2p}{n}}\right)=\arctan\left(\frac{1}{2p}\pm\sqrt{n}-\frac{1}{2pn}\right),

we get

\displaystyle\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x)\,dx=\left[D_{p,n}(x)\right]_{p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}}^{p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{p^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{2\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\arctan\left(\frac{1}{2p}+\sqrt{n}-\frac{1}{2pn}\right)-\arctan\left(\frac{1}{2p}-\sqrt{n}-\frac{1}{2pn}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\cdot\left(-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=\frac{\pi}{2}.\qed

Lemma B10
.

Let
\varphi:{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{C}}
be a measurable function with

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\varphi(x)\right|\,dx<\infty.

Further, let
p\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
be a Lebesgue point of
\varphi
. Then

\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx=\frac{1}{2}\varphi(p).

Proof.

For
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
with
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}<p
set

D_{n}\coloneqq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x)\,dx.

Then, by Lemma
B9
, we have
\lim_{n\to\infty}D_{n}=\frac{1}{2}
. Further, we define

k_{n}(x)\coloneqq\frac{\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x)}{\pi D_{n}}.

Then

\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}k_{n}(x)\,dx=1

and
k_{n}(x)\geq 0
for
x\in\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
.
Then, for sufficiently large
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
, one has

\displaystyle k_{n}\left(p\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{p\left(p\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\frac{2p^{2}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{\left(\left(p\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{p\left(p+1\right)}{n\cdot p^{2}\cdot 1}\cdot\frac{4p^{2}}{\left(\frac{1}{n}\pm\frac{2p}{\sqrt{n}}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{4p\left(p+1\right)}{\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\pm 2p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{3}}}\right)^{2}}\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}\frac{2}{\pi}\frac{4p\left(p+1\right)}{4p^{2}}=\frac{2(p+1)}{\pi p},

so for sufficiently large
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
, we have

k_{n}\left(p\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\leq\frac{p+1}{p}\leq\frac{p+1}{p}\sqrt{n}.

Further, we have

\displaystyle k_{n}^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\frac{x}{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}-\frac{2x^{2}\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)

For
x\in\left(p-\frac{1}{n},p+\frac{1}{n}\right)
, using

\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\leq\frac{4p^{3}}{n\cdot p^{2}\cdot 1}=\frac{4p}{n}

and

\displaystyle\left|x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left(p+(x-p)\right)^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right|=\left|2p(x-p)+(x-p)^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2p\frac{1}{n}+\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\leq\frac{2(p+1)}{n},

this yields

\displaystyle\left|k_{n}^{\prime}(x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{4p}{n}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}+\frac{2x^{2}\left|x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right|}{\left(\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{4p}{n}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}+\frac{2(p+1)^{2}\cdot\frac{2(p+1)}{n}}{\left(4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\left(\frac{n}{p}+\frac{(p+1)^{3}n^{2}}{p^{3}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2(p+1)^{3}}{p^{3}}\cdot n^{2}.

Then the fact that
\lim_{n\to\infty}D_{n}=\frac{1}{2}
implies that there is
r>0
such that
\left|k_{n}^{\prime}(x)\right|\leq rn^{2}
for every
x\in\left(p-\frac{1}{n},p+\frac{1}{n}\right)
.

Further, for
x\in\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p-\frac{1}{n}\right)
, we have

\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\leq 0,

so

\displaystyle k_{n}^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}-\frac{2x^{2}\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(0-0\right)=0.

For
x\in\left(p+\frac{1}{n},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)
, we get

\displaystyle\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}-2x^{2}\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)=\left(-x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\left(p^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}-x^{4}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\leq\left(p^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}-\left(p+\frac{1}{n}\right)^{4}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\left(p^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}-\left(p^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}+2\frac{p}{n}\right)^{2}+\left(2\frac{p}{n}\right)^{2}\leq 0,

so

\displaystyle k_{n}^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}}-\frac{2x^{2}\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\frac{\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}-2x^{2}\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\pi D_{n}}\frac{2p^{3}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+1\right)}{n\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}+p^{2}\right)\left(1+\frac{p^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)}\cdot\left(\frac{0}{\left(\left(x^{2}-p^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}+4p^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)^{2}}\right)=0.

These estimates show that we can apply Lemma
B8
and get

\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}\widetilde{d}_{p,n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx=D_{n}\cdot\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}k_{n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{2}\cdot\varphi(p).\qed

Lemma B11
.

Let
\varphi:{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{C}}
be a measurable function with

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\varphi(x)\right|\,dx<\infty.

Further, let
p\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
such that
p
and
-p
are Lebesgue points of
\varphi
. Then

\frac{\varphi(p)+\varphi(-p)}{2}=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}d_{p,n}(x)\,\varphi(x)\,dx.

Proof.

By Lemma
B10
and Lemma
B6
, we have

\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}d_{p,n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx=\frac{1}{2}\varphi(p).

Since for every
p\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
and every
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
the function
d_{p,n}
is symmetric, we then also get

\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}d_{p,n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx=\frac{1}{2}\varphi(-p),

so

\frac{\varphi(p)+\varphi(-p)}{2}=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}d_{p,n}(x)\,\varphi(x)\,dx.\qed

Theorem B12
.

(Lebesgue Differentiation Theorem)
(cf.
[
Le10
]
)

Let
f\in L^{1}_{\mathrm{loc}}({\mathbb{R}},{\mathbb{C}})
. Then almost every
p\in{\mathbb{R}}
is a Lebesgue point of
f
.

Corollary B13
.

For any measurable function
\varphi:{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{C}}
with

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\varphi(x)\right|\,dx<\infty

almost every
p\in{\mathbb{R}}
is a Lebesgue point of
\varphi
.

Proof.

For
0<a<b<\infty
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{[-b,-a]\cup[a,b]}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{[-b,-a]\cup[a,b]}\frac{\left(1+b^{2}\right)^{2}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(1+b^{2}\right)^{2}}{a^{2}}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\varphi(x)\right|\,dx<\infty.

The statement then follows by Theorem
B12
.
∎

Theorem B14
.

For any measurable function
\varphi:{\mathbb{R}}\to{\mathbb{C}}
with

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}\left|\varphi(x)\right|\,dx<\infty

one has

\frac{\varphi(p)+\varphi(-p)}{2}=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}f_{p,n}(x)\,\varphi(x)\,dx

for almost every
p\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\times}
.

Proof.

Let
p\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}
such that
p
and
-p
are Lebesgue points of
\varphi
. For
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
, we set

c_{n}\coloneqq\int_{\left[-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},-1\right)\cup\left(1,p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right]}g_{n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx\quad\text{if }p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\geq 1

and

c_{n}\coloneqq-\int_{\left[-1,-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},1\right]}g_{n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx\quad\text{if }p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}<1.

Then, for
p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\geq 1
, one has

\displaystyle|c_{n}|\leq\int_{\left[-p-1,-1\right)\cup\left(1,p+1\right]}\frac{1}{n}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\left[-p-1,-1\right)\cup\left(1,p+1\right]}\frac{1}{n}\frac{\left(1+\left(p+1\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{1^{2}}\cdot\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(1+\left(p+1\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{n}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}0

and for
p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}<1
, one has

\displaystyle|c_{n}|\leq\int_{\left[-1,-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},1\right]}\frac{1}{n}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\left[-1,-p\right)\cup\left(p,1\right]}\frac{1}{n}\frac{\left(1+\left(1\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{p^{2}}\cdot\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{np^{2}}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}0,

so in every case

\lim_{n\to\infty}c_{n}=0.

Further, for
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
with
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\frac{p}{2}
, we set

k_{n}\coloneqq\int_{\left[-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},-1\right)\cup\left(1,p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right]}g_{n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx\quad\text{if }p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\geq 1

and

k_{n}\coloneqq-\int_{\left[-1,-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},1\right]}g_{n}(x)\varphi(x)\,dx\quad\text{if }p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}<1.

Then, for
p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\geq 1
, one has

\displaystyle|k_{n}|\leq\int_{\left[-p,-1\right)\cup\left(1,p\right]}\frac{1}{n}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\left[-p,-1\right)\cup\left(1,p\right]}\frac{1}{n}\frac{\left(1+p^{2}\right)^{2}}{1^{2}}\cdot\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(1+p^{2}\right)^{2}}{n}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}0

and for
p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}<1
, one has

\displaystyle|k_{n}|\leq\int_{\left[-1,-\frac{p}{2}\right)\cup\left(\frac{p}{2},1\right]}\frac{1}{n}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\left[-1,-\frac{p}{2}\right)\cup\left(\frac{p}{2},1\right]}\frac{1}{n}\frac{\left(1+\left(1\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{p}{2}\right)^{2}}\cdot\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16}{np^{2}}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{x^{2}}{\left(1+x^{2}\right)^{2}}|\varphi(x)|\,dx\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}0,

so, in every case

\lim_{n\to\infty}k_{n}=0.

Then, by Lemma
B3
, Lemma
B4
and Lemma
B11
, one has

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}f_{p,n}(x)\,\varphi(x)\,dx

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}d_{p,n}(x)\,\varphi(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad+\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(-p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}\left(d_{p,n}(x)+\frac{g_{n}(x)}{p^{2}}\right)\varphi(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad+\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\left(-\infty,-p-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\cup\left(p+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\infty\right)}\left(d_{p,n}(x)+g_{n}(x)\right)\varphi(x)\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad+\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}c_{n}+\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi}\frac{k_{n}}{p^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\varphi(p)+\varphi(-p)}{2}+0+0+0+0

\displaystyle=\frac{\varphi(p)+\varphi(-p)}{2}.

The assertion then follows, since by Corollary
B13
almost every
p\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\times}
is a Lebesgue point of the function
\varphi
.
∎
